[2m│[0m [2m│[0m Randomizes a plugin's parameters, saves its state, recreates the
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m plugin instance, reloads the state, and then checks whether the
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m parameter values are the same and whether saving the state once
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m more results in the same parameters as before. The parameter
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m values are updated using the process function.

[2m│[0m [2m┌[0m[2m─[0m [1mstate-reproducibility-binary[0m
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m Performs the same parameter reproducibility check as in 'state-
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m reproducibility-basic', but also checks that the saved state data
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m is exactly the same byte for byte. This means that the plugin
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m needs to save the state in a completely deterministic way, without
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m any non-determinism coming from things like uninitialized memory
[2m│[0m [2m│[0m or random bytes.
